We propose COAST, a context-aware differential learning framework for spatial gene expression prediction. COAST conditions the local and global context features with type-specific modulation and aggregates the target and context spot tokens using a Transformer encoder to capture both fine-grained local patterns and slide-level structure. It is trained with a joint objective that combines absolute expression regression with signed differential regression between the target and context spots. Experiments on multiple spatial transcriptomics datasets show consistent improvements in correlation- and distribution-based metrics, demonstrating the effectiveness of context-aware differential learning for histology-based spatial gene expression prediction.

</details>


### [73] [Understanding Schedule-Free Methods in Nonconvex Optimization: Rate Guarantees and Escaping Saddles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09167)
*Jiseok Chae,Donghwan Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Schedule-Free梯度下降和随机梯度下降方法在非凸光滑优化中达到最优收敛速率，并证明了严格鞍点回避性质，为其强经验性能提供了理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schedule-Free方法在实验中表现优异，甚至超越调参后的调度器，但其在非凸优化中的收敛理论尚属空白，本文旨在填补这一理论缺口。

Method: 基于连续时间极限常微分方程的Lyapunov分析，证明Schedule-Free GD/SGD在标准形式下的最优最坏情况收敛率；并将其建模为非自治动力系统证明严格鞍点回避。

Result: Schedule-Free GD和SGD达到一阶方法中可达的最优最坏情况收敛率；在任意小单次扰动下可回避严格鞍点。

Conclusion: 理论结果解释了Schedule-Free方法强经验性能的原因，为非凸优化中免调度方法提供了严格理论基础。

Abstract: Schedule-Free methods have attracted growing interest for alleviating the burden of designing and tuning a learning rate scheduler, while matching and sometimes even outperforming optimizers with tuned schedulers. Despite their strong empirical results, their convergence theory in nonconvex optimization, where modern machine learning objectives typically arise, has remained largely unexplored. In this paper, we provide worst-case analyses of Schedule-Free gradient descent and Schedule-Free stochastic gradient descent, in their standard form and without auxiliary modifications or restrictive conditions, for smooth but possibly nonconvex objectives. Based on a Lyapunov analysis derived from the continuous-time limiting ordinary differential equation associated with these methods, we show that Schedule-Free gradient descent and Schedule-Free stochastic gradient descent achieve the optimal worst-case convergence rates attainable among first-order methods. We further formulate Schedule-Free gradient descent as a nonautonomous dynamical system and prove strict-saddle avoidance under an arbitrarily small one-time perturbation. These theoretical results provide a better understanding of the strong performance that Schedule-Free methods demonstrate.

</details>


### [74] [Application of machine learning to monster level prediction in tabletop RPG game design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09196)
*Jolanta Śliwa,Jakub Adamczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何利用机器学习预测《开拓者》第二版中怪物的等级（ordinal level），将其建模为表格序数回归任务。实验表明，基于树的集成模型在时序评估协议下表现接近完美，且可解释性分析显示模型与人类设计师直觉和游戏规则高度一致，可作为TTRPG怪物平衡设计的辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在桌面角色扮演游戏（如《开拓者》）开发中，设计平衡的怪物是一项核心但劳动密集型的任务，每个怪物由多个数值属性共同决定其战力（以序数等级表示）。当前缺乏自动化的工具来辅助设计师进行等级预测和平衡性调整。

Method: 首次构建了专门用于TTRPG怪物等级预测的数据集（来自《开拓者》第二版公开数据）。将任务建模为表格序数回归，比较了经典回归模型（含取整策略）、专用表格序数回归算法以及带序数感知损失的神经网络。所有模型在时序协议（chronological and expanding-window protocols）下使用多种互补指标进行评估，并进行了可解释AI分析。

Result: 基于树的集成模型（如随机森林、梯度提升树）在时序评估下表现最优，实现了接近完美的序数排名和高预测准确率，显著优于线性模型和神经方法。可解释性分析表明模型与人类直觉和游戏规则一致。

Conclusion: 机器学习能够可靠地近似设计师对怪物等级的判断，可作为有效的计算机辅助工具用于怪物平衡性和更广泛的TTRPG系统设计。

Abstract: Designing balanced adversaries is a central but labor-intensive task in tabletop role-playing game (TTRPG) development. In systems such as Pathfinder, each monster is described by many numerical attributes that jointly determine its power, summarized as an ordinal level. We investigate whether machine learning can support designers by predicting this level from a monster's attributes, framing the task as tabular ordinal regression. We introduce what is, to our knowledge, the first dataset built specifically for TTRPG monster-level prediction, derived from publicly available Pathfinder Second Edition data. Using it, we compare classical regression models with rounding schemes, dedicated tabular ordinal regression algorithms, and neural networks with ordinal-aware losses. To mirror real design workflows, we evaluate all models under chronological and expanding-window protocols with several complementary metrics. Results show that tree-based ensembles outperform linear models and neural approaches, achieving near-perfect ordinal ranking and high predictive accuracy. Explainable AI analyses, such as feature importance and error distributions, show that the model is aligned with human intuition and follows patterns grounded in game rules. Together, these results show that machine learning can reliably approximate designer judgments and serve as an effective computer-aided tool for monster balancing and broader TTRPG system design.

</details>


### [75] [Interference and Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09202)
*Julius Störk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出遗忘应被视为任务间的干扰，并基于此推导出无重放、无Fisher的IGFA方法，在任务可分时实现无损保留，不可分时将不可逆遗忘转化为可恢复的可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法依赖重放、弹性正则化或蒸馏等后处理机制，未能直接将遗忘建模为任务间的干扰。

Method: 在冻结特征机制下，遗忘等价于旧任务的干扰能量；在深度网络中通过路径平均曲率恢复该量。基于此几何分析，提出Interference-Gated Functional Allocation (IGFA)，一种无重放、无Fisher的方法，在任务对齐时共享方向，冲突时保护方向。

Result: 当任务结构可分时实现无损保留；当任务不可分时，将不可避免代价从不可逆遗忘转化为可恢复的可塑性。在不相似任务流上匹配最强无重放结构基线，在相似性值得保留时优于无条件的投影方法。

Conclusion: 通过将遗忘建模为任务间干扰，IGFA能够根据任务几何关系自适应分配参数，在无需重放和Fisher信息的条件下平衡保留与可塑性。

Abstract: Continual learning commonly relies on post-hoc mechanisms such as replay, elastic regularization, or distillation. This work argues that forgetting should instead be modeled directly as interference between tasks. In the frozen-feature regime, forgetting from learning a new task is exactly the interference energy induced on the old task. In deep networks, the same quantity is recovered through path-averaged curvature with minimal additional forward passes.
  When task supports are disjoint, forgetting can be eliminated structurally and when task supports overlap in conflicting directions, a non-zero distortion floor is unavoidable. The same geometry optimally merges models through task-aware orthogonalization. From this analysis we derive Interference-Gated Functional Allocation (IGFA), a replay-free, Fisher-free method that shares directions when tasks align and protects them when they conflict. Across benchmarks, IGFA achieves lossless retention when tasks are structurally separable and moves unavoidable cost from irreversible forgetting into deferred but recoverable plasticity when they are not. It matches the strongest replay-free structural baselines on dissimilar-task streams and improves on unconditional projection when similarity makes transfer worth preserving.

</details>


### [76] [Temporal Knowledge Graph Forecasting under Distribution Shifts: A Synthetic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09232)
*Konrad Özdemir,Julia Gastinger,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过合成时间知识图谱生成器，系统研究了七种预测模型在受控分布偏移环境下的鲁棒性，发现模型鲁棒性高度依赖信号类型——基于重复性和周期性的规律在平稳条件下容易恢复，但潜在实体-社区结构的变化是最具挑战性的偏移类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间知识图谱预测模型仅在经验基准数据集上评估，无法揭示模型对分布偏移的鲁棒性。为此，作者提出在受控偏移环境下系统研究TKG预测模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用合成TKG生成器编码三种时序与结构属性（重复性、同质性和周期性）作为数据生成机制，在平稳和偏移两种机制下评估七种预测架构。

Result: 1) 基于重复性和周期性的规律在平稳条件下可恢复；2) 简单记忆基线在重复性主导数据时有竞争力；3) 结构断裂暴露模型适应能力局限，潜在实体-社区结构变化是最强挑战。

Conclusion: 加深了对现有TKG模型面对时间分布偏移时能力与局限的理解。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) represent evolving relational systems, whose underlying data-generating processes often change over time. Yet, TKG forecasting models are commonly evaluated only on empirical benchmark datasets that provide limited insight into the models' robustness to such distribution shifts. Recognising this issue, we study TKG forecasting under controlled shift environments using a synthetic TKG generator that encodes three temporal and structural properties -- recurrence, homophily, and periodicity -- as data-generating mechanisms. This allows us to evaluate seven forecasting architectures under stationary and shifting regimes. Our experiments suggest that robustness in TKG forecasting is highly signal-dependent. Recurrence-based and periodic regularities are largely recoverable under stationary conditions, and simple memory-based baselines can be competitive when recurrence dominates the data. However, structural breaks reveal limitations in model adaptivity, with shifts in latent entity-community structure posing the strongest challenge in our study. Overall, our findings improve the understanding of the capabilities and limitations of current TKG models confronted with temporal distribution shifts.

</details>


### [77] [All you need is SAMPAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09235)
*Jayadeva,Madhur Aswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SAMPAT的三层神经网络架构，能够学习连续可微函数并通过闭式代数/解析表达式实现完全可解释性，同时在不同约束下可退化为多种经典模型（如多项式、高斯混合等），性能与现有方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI/ML最先进方法依赖深度神经网络，但普遍缺乏可解释性。在科学实验数据分析中，仅靠定量预测不足以为科学家提供洞察，可解释性至关重要。

Method: 提出SAMPAT（光滑多元多项式与解析变换逼近）三层神经网络架构，可证明能学习任意光滑函数的连续可微逼近，输出为闭式紧致的代数/解析表达式。通过限制神经元连接可退化为正则多项式、三角多项式、有理表达式、高斯及高斯混合等模型；无限制时自主学习合适结构。加入跳跃连接后，4-6层SAMPAT可表示AI/ML中广泛使用的方法。

Result: 在合成数据集和基准数据集上的实验表明，SAMPAT能以更简洁的表示取得有竞争力的性能。对许多任务，两层SAMPAT即足够。SAMPAT还可用于多项式分解和非线性系统建模。

Conclusion: SAMPAT实现了模型家族（而非仅参数）在学习过程中的优化选择，为构建可解释神经网络提供了新的理论框架和实用方法。

Abstract: The current state of the art in AI/ML rests on deep neural architectures, which, in general, suffer from a lack of interpretability. Interpretability is crucial to gleaning insights while analyzing experimental data, where quantitative predictions may not be adequate for a scientist. We present a three layer neural architecture, SAMPAT (Smooth Approximation via Multivariate Polynomials and Analytic Transformations), that can provably learn a continuous, everywhere differentiable function, that can approximate any smooth function arbitrarily closely. SAMPAT's approximant can be expressed as a closed and compact algebraic, analytic expression, providing complete interpretability. Experiments on synthetic and benchmark datasets indicate that SAMPAT yields competitive performance with simpler representations. For many tasks, a two layer SAMPAT suffices. By imposing restrictions on the connectivity between neurons, SAMPAT may be used to provide a range of approximants, including regular and trigonometric polynomials, rational expressions, Gaussians, mixtures of Gaussians, as well as arbitrary combinations of the same; without restrictions, it learns a suitable structure. SAMPAT may be used to factorize polynomials and model nonlinear systems. With the addition of skip connections, a 4 to 6 layer SAMPAT is adequate to represent a substantive range of methods widely used in AI/ML, allowing the choice of a model's family, not just its parameters, to also be optimized as part of the learning process.

</details>


### [78] [Risk-Aware General-Utility Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09298)
*Pedro P. Santos,Fábio Vital,Alberto Sardinha,Francisco S. Melo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有风险意识的通用效用马尔可夫决策过程（GUMDPs），提出使用熵风险度量（ERM）作为目标，并基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）设计在线规划算法，可任意精度求解该问题。实验验证了该方法在标准MDP、最大状态熵探索、模仿学习和多目标MDP等多种任务中优化风险感知行为的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUMDP框架虽然能表达丰富的目标函数，但缺乏对风险意识的建模。现实决策中，决策者往往需要在期望性能和风险规避之间进行权衡，因此需要将风险度量引入GUMDP框架。

Method: 首先形式化定义了风险感知GUMDPs，以熵风险度量（ERM）为具体风险目标；然后提出基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的在线规划方法，理论上可证明能求解至任意期望精度。

Result: 实验在标准MDP、最大状态熵探索、模仿学习和多目标MDP等多样任务上验证了所提方法能够成功优化一系列风险感知行为。

Conclusion: 将风险意识引入GUMDP框架拓展了其应用范围，MCTS方法能够高效求解该问题，为实际决策中期望性能与风险规避的权衡提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We study general-utility Markov decision processes (GUMDPs) with risk-aware objectives. In this framework, an agent aims to optimize a risk measure of the distribution of objective values, where the objective function depends on the frequency of visitation of states induced by the agent's policy. First, we motivate, propose, and formalize risk-aware GUMDPs, which enable agents and decision makers to trade off expected performance by risk aversion while benefiting from the rich set of objectives that can be cast under the framework of GUMDPs. We focus our attention on the entropic risk measure (ERM). Second, we show how we can solve risk-aware GUMDPs with ERM objectives by resorting to online planning techniques. In particular, we propose an approach based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to provably solve risk-aware GUMDPs up to any desired accuracy. Third, we provide a set of experimental results showcasing that our approach is successful when optimizing for a spectrum of risk-aware behaviors in the context of GUMDPs under diverse tasks (standard MDPs, maximum state entropy exploration, imitation learning, and multi-objective MDPs).

</details>


### [79] [Forget Narrowly, Retain Broadly: Unlearning as an Asymmetric Generalization Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09236)
*Amit Peleg,Naman Deep Singh,Naama Pearl,Bibhabasu Mohapatra,Matthias Hein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SUITE是一个针对LLM机器遗忘的评估协议和训练语料库，解决了现有基准测试中遗忘不足（under-forgetting）和过度遗忘（over-forgetting）的不对称泛化问题，并提出了JensUn++算法，在三个LLM上实现了最佳的遗忘-保留效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘基准测试不可靠：一方面无法检测到通过改写或间接查询重新浮现的知识（遗忘不足），另一方面缺乏语义、句法和词汇层面的探针来验证无关知识是否被保留（过度遗忘）。这两种失败反映了不对称泛化问题。

Method: 提出SUITE评估协议和训练语料库，捕捉真实世界事实领域中遗忘-保留的结构边界；基于此洞察提出JensUn++遗忘算法。

Result: 基于SUITE训练的方法在遗忘效果上显著提升；JensUn++在三个LLM的序列和联合遗忘设置中均达到最佳遗忘-保留效用权衡。

Conclusion: 训练数据与算法设计同等重要；SUITE为机器遗忘提供了更可靠的评估基础。

Abstract: Machine unlearning in LLMs is the targeted removal of specific knowledge while preserving all other capabilities, critical for privacy and safety. Yet existing benchmarks measure it unreliably. They miss knowledge that resurfaces under paraphrased or indirect queries, a failure we call under-forgetting, and lack the semantic, syntactic, and lexical probes needed to verify that unrelated knowledge is preserved, a failure we call over-forgetting. Both failures reflect an asymmetric generalization problem. Forget evaluation must cover diverse query formulations of the same target facts, testing whether forgetting holds beyond exact training prompts. Retain evaluation must probe a far larger and implicitly defined set, namely every fact disjoint from the forget target. The retain set thus defines the effective forget set, yet current datasets provide no fine-grained annotation of this forget-retain boundary. We address this with SUITE, an evaluation protocol and training corpus that captures forget-retain structure for real-world factual domains. Methods trained on SUITE improve substantially, showing that training data is as important as algorithmic design. Building on the obtained insights, we introduce JensUn++, an unlearning algorithm that achieves the best forget-retain utility trade-off across three LLMs, in both sequential and joint unlearning settings. Code and datasets are available at https://amitpeleg.github.io/forget-narrowly-retain-broadly

</details>


### [80] [LionVote: Per-Layer Learning Rate Adaptation for Lion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09266)
*Kris Atallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LionVote，一种逐层学习率调节机制，通过梯度方向稳定性和动量健康度两个诊断指标为每层投票调整学习率，在ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100上达到69.7%准确率，优于Lion（69.0%）和AdamW（68.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lion优化器在不同层类型（注意力/MLP vs 归一化层）之间存在高达32%的有效学习率尺度差异，单一全局学习率无法适应这种跨层类型的不匹配，因此需要逐层自适应学习率机制。

Method: 提出LionVote机制：每层参数张量维护一个复合整数（compound level），每c个epoch由两个诊断指标（梯度方向稳定性、动量健康度）更新，平局时用验证集损失裁决；投票阈值基于几何恒等式、EMA时间常数和噪声底限估计；更新节奏由结构边界约束并经消融实验选定。

Result: ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100上LionVote达到69.7% top-1准确率，显著优于Lion的69.0%（p<0.02, Welch's t-test）和AdamW的68.8%；逐层自适应价值取决于架构异质性和任务：均匀CNN架构搭配余弦退火SGD仍占优，ViT架构上收益具有任务依赖性。

Conclusion: LionVote通过逐层学习率调节有效解决了Lion优化器跨层类型的尺度不匹配问题，但自适应机制的价值并非普适，需根据架构和任务特性评估适用性。

Abstract: Per-layer diagnostics reveal that, at the prescribed learning rate, Lion's effective scale is 2.6-2.8x too high for attention and MLP parameters and ~2x too high for normalization layers on ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100; this 32% cross-layer-type disparity cannot be reproduced by a single global rate. The measurement comes from LionVote, a per-layer learning rate mechanism in which each parameter tensor maintains a compound level, a persistent integer updated every c epochs by two diagnostics (gradient direction stability and momentum health) resolved by a validation loss tiebreaker. Voting thresholds derive from geometric identities, the EMA time constant, and a noise-floor estimate; cadence is bounded structurally and selected by ablation. On ViT-Tiny/CIFAR-100, LionVote achieves 69.7% top-1 accuracy vs. Lion's 69.0% (p < 0.02, Welch's t-test) and AdamW's 68.8%. Per-layer adaptation value depends on both architectural heterogeneity and task; on uniform CNN architectures tuned SGD with cosine annealing remains dominant, and on ViT architectures gains are task-dependent.

</details>


### [81] [Autoregressive latent diffusion for 3D molecule generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09277)
*Federico Ottomano,Gaopeng Ren,Yingzhen Li,Kim E. Jelfs,Alex M. Ganose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KRONOS是一种潜在自回归扩散框架，结合自回归生成与扩散模型优势，通过预训练自编码器的潜在空间建模分子图拓扑和几何，并采用Fill-in-the-Middle混合训练策略，在单一自回归模型中同时支持无条件生成和片段条件生成，在QM9和GEOM-Drugs数据集上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D分子生成方法中，扩散模型需要预先指定分子大小，而自回归方法在条件生成和无条件生成之间难以平衡。作者希望设计一种框架，既能支持变长生成和片段条件生成，又能保持与扩散模型相当的无条件生成质量。

Method: KRONOS框架包含三个关键设计：1）在预训练自编码器的潜在空间中进行自回归生成；2）联合建模分子图的拓扑结构和几何结构；3）采用受Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) 范式启发的混合训练策略，使同一个从左到右的自回归模型既能进行无条件生成，也能进行片段条件生成。

Result: 在QM9和GEOM-Drugs数据集上，KRONOS在无条件生成任务中达到自回归方法中的领先水平，与扩散模型竞争力相当。片段条件生成对无条件生成性能的影响可以忽略不计，证明单一架构可以同时支持两种生成范式。

Conclusion: KRONOS成功弥合了自回归方法与扩散模型在3D分子生成上的性能差距，同时在一个统一的框架内实现了无条件生成和片段条件生成两种范式，为分子生成领域提供了一种更灵活高效的方案。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) molecule generation has been dominated by diffusion models, which achieve strong generation quality but typically require the molecular size to be specified a priori. Recent autoregressive approaches have substantially narrowed the performance gap while naturally supporting variable-length generation and conditioning on partial molecular context. However, balancing unconditional and context-conditioned generation remains challenging. We introduce KRONOS, a latent autoregressive diffusion framework that generates molecules in the latent space of a pre-trained autoencoder, jointly modeling molecular graph topology and geometry, while retaining the flexibility of autoregressive generation. We further introduce a mixed training strategy inspired by Fill-in-the Middle (FIM) paradigm, enabling both unconditional and fragment-conditioned molecular generation within a single left-to-right autoregressive model. Experiments on QM9 and GEOM-Drugs demonstrate that KRONOS achieves leading unconditional generation performance among autoregressive methods, while remaining competitive with diffusion models. Moreover, fragment-conditioned generation is achieved with negligible impact on unconditional generation performance, demonstrating that both generation paradigms can be supported within a single architecture.

</details>


### [82] [Shortcut Trajectory Planning for Efficient Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09336)
*Guanquan Wang,Yoshimasa Tsuruoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STP是一个离线模型强化学习框架，通过单阶段训练的捷径轨迹模型实现高效轨迹生成，支持可调节的一步/少步推理，在D4RL基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的轨迹规划器推理成本高，而一致性规划器虽减少采样步数，但依赖两阶段师生蒸馏流水线，增加了训练成本且可能引入不稳定性。

Method: STP框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 单阶段训练的条件捷径轨迹模型；(2) 通过步长条件化支持可调节的一步和少步推理；(3) 结合可行性感知校正的评论家进行候选计划选择。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试的 locomotion、navigation、manipulation 和 dexterous control 任务上均取得了强劲性能。

Conclusion: STP通过简化训练流水线实现了快速生成规划，兼顾了高效推理与强性能表现。

Abstract: Diffusion-based trajectory planners have shown strong performance in offline reinforcement learning, but their iterative denoising process often incurs high inference cost. Consistency-based planners reduce the number of sampling steps, yet they typically rely on a two-stage teacher--student distillation pipeline that increases training cost and may introduce instability. We propose Shortcut Trajectory Planning (STP), an offline model-based reinforcement learning framework that incorporates shortcut models as efficient trajectory generators. STP trains a conditional shortcut trajectory model in a single stage, supports adjustable one-step and few-step inference through step-size conditioning, and selects candidate plans using a critic augmented with feasibility-aware correction. Across standard D4RL benchmarks, including locomotion, navigation, manipulation, and dexterous control tasks, STP achieves strong performance while simplifying the training pipeline for fast generative planning.

</details>


### [83] [Graph Neural Networks for Scalable and Transferable Node Centrality Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09372)
*Samra Sana,Giorgio Mantica,Saul Imbrici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究使用图神经网络（GNN）来近似计算介数中心性和紧密度中心性，将其建模为节点排序问题。在未见过的Erdos-Renyi图上，模型达到了介数τ=0.851和紧密度τ=0.894的Kendall秩相关系数；在大规模图上（N=5000）介数τ达0.938；混合分布训练提升了跨图族迁移能力；GNN推理相比精确计算实现了高达97.7倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图中心性指标（介数、紧密度）的精确计算在大规模图上计算代价高昂，需要一种可扩展的近似方法。本文探索利用GNN的学习能力来高效逼近这些中心性指标，实现节点排序。

Method: 将中心性近似建模为监督节点排序问题，使用精确中心性值作为监督信号。采用消息传递GNN架构，在不同图拓扑（Erdos-Renyi、Barabasi-Albert、Gaussian Random Partition）上进行训练和测试，评估Kendall tau秩相关系数，并研究跨图族迁移能力。

Result: 在未见过Erdos-Renyi图上介数τ=0.851、紧密度τ=0.894；大规模图（N=5000）介数τ=0.938；GNN推理加速比达97.7倍；混合分布训练改善了介数中心性跨图族迁移，但紧密度中心性对社区结构图敏感，向真实拓扑迁移受限。

Conclusion: 混合分布训练可以提升GNN中心性近似的结构迁移能力，但紧密度中心性对拓扑的敏感性仍是一个开放挑战。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) provide a learning-based framework for approximating graph quantities that are expensive to compute exactly. This paper investigates GNNs for scalable approximation of betweenness and closeness centrality, formulated as a node-ranking problem. Exact centrality values are used as supervision, and ranking quality is evaluated using Kendall's tau rank correlation. We study whether message-passing GNNs can learn transferable structural representations across different graph topologies rather than only fitting the distribution used during training. On unseen Erdos renyi graphs, the proposed models achieve tau = 0.851 for betweenness and tau = 0.894 for closeness. A large-scale betweenness model trained on graphs with N = 5,000 nodes achieves tau = 0.938, demonstrating scalability. Mixed-distribution training on Erdos renyi, Barabasi-Albert, and Gaussian Random Partition graphs improves betweenness transfer across graph families. In contrast, closeness centrality remains more sensitive to community-structured graphs and shows reduced transfer to real-world topologies. Finally, GNN inference achieves up to a 97.7x speedup over exact computation. These results show that mixed-distribution training can improve structural transfer in GNN-based centrality approximation, while identifying closeness centrality's sensitivity to topology as an open challenge.

</details>


### [84] [Fully Trainable Deep Differentiable Logic Gate Networks and Lookup Table Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09399)
*Wout Mommen,Lars Keuninckx,Matthias Hartmann,Werner Van Leekwijck,Piet Wambacq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对深度可微逻辑门网络（LGN）和查找表网络（LUTN）的连接优化训练方法，通过在每个门/LUT输入引脚上使用概率分布选择最优连接，同时并行学习最优门类型或LUT条目。该方法在Yin-Yang、MNIST和Fashion-MNIST基准上显著超越了固定连接LGN，在MNIST上使用两层8000个门达到98.92%准确率，且门数量减少近50倍。此外还提出了支持反向传播稳定训练的LUT神经元描述，在6层深度网络中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定连接LGN和LUTN在连接结构上缺乏灵活性，限制了模型表达能力和效率。现有方法无法在训练过程中动态优化连接，导致需要大量逻辑门才能达到理想性能。本文旨在通过可微的连接选择机制，同时优化连接结构和门类型/LUT条目，以更少的资源实现更高精度。

Method: 在每个门或LUT输入引脚上维护一个连接候选集的概率分布，通过前向传播选择merit最高的连接，反向传播使用直通估计器（STE）和高温学习率实现稳定训练。对于LGN，同时学习最优门类型；对于LUTN，同时学习最优LUT条目。引入修剪常数输出门类型的策略来增强深层网络训练的稳定性。

Result: MNIST数据集上：两层8000个门达98.92%，单层8000个门达98.45%，门数量比固定连接LGN减少约50倍。LUTN上：两层2000个6输入LUT达98.88%。相比固定连接LGN训练算法，参数减少4倍且精度更高。在Yin-Yang和Fashion-MNIST上同样表现优异。

Conclusion: 所提出的连接优化方法能有效提升LGN和LUTN的性能与效率，以更少的逻辑门和参数实现更高精度，并且通过适当的训练策略（高学习率、STE、门类型修剪）保证了深层网络（至10层）的训练稳定性。

Abstract: We introduce a novel method for both partial and full optimization of the connections in deep differentiable logic gate networks (LGNs) and lookup table networks (LUTNs). Our training method utilizes a probability distribution over a set of connections per gate/lookup table (LUT) input pin, selecting the connection with highest merit, all whilst the optimal gate types or LUT-entries are learned in parallel. We show that the connection-optimized LGNs outperform standard fixed-connection LGNs on the Yin-Yang, MNIST Handwritten Digits and Fashion-MNIST benchmarks, while requiring only a fraction of the number of logic gates. We achieve 98.92% on the MNIST dataset with two layers of 8000 gates. With only one layer of 8000 gates, we obtain 98.45%, showing that our method requires almost 50 times fewer gates compared to fixed-connection LGNs. Training stability up to ten layers has been ensured by employing a high learning rate, straight-through estimators and trimming constant-output gate types. Additionally, we present a LUT neuron description that enables stable training with backpropagation, tested up to 6-layer deep networks. The model requires four times fewer trainable parameters and still achieves a higher accuracy compared to the fixed-connection LGN training algorithm. Our connection-training algorithm also works well for the LUTNs, achieving an accuracy of 98.88% for two layers of 2000 6-input LUTs.

</details>


### [85] [Learning Physics-Informed Surrogate Model of Linear Elastic Displacement Fields from Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09382)
*Rodolphe Barlogis,Ferhat Tamssaouet,Quentin Falcoz,Stéphane Grieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的DeepONet框架，用于含裂缝弹性体的实时结构健康监测。该代理模型能够根据边界条件和裂缝几何预测位移场，无需有限元生成训练数据，并通过局部罚函数弱施加裂缝边界无牵引条件。数值实验证明了该方法的可行性，为扩展到多种裂缝几何的代理建模奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种快速且物理一致的代理模型，用于含裂缝弹性域的结构健康监测，实现实时监测能力。

Method: 采用物理信息DeepONet框架，设计专门的编码策略处理裂缝几何信息，通过局部罚项弱施加裂缝边界上的无牵引条件，不依赖有限元生成训练数据。

Result: 针对一种代表性裂缝几何的数值示例，验证了该公式的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为扩展到多种裂缝几何的代理建模奠定了基础，有望实现实时结构健康监测。

Abstract: This work aims to develop a fast and physically consistent surrogate model for real-time structural health monitoring of fractured elastic domains. We propose a physics-informed DeepONet framework that predicts displacement fields from both boundary conditions and fracture geometry, using a dedicated encoding strategy for the latter and without relying on finite-element-generated training data. The traction-free condition on the fracture boundary is imposed weakly through a localized penalty term. The presented numerical example focuses on one representative fracture geometry, demonstrating the feasibility of the formulation and laying the groundwork for extensions to surrogate modeling across diverse fracture geometries.

</details>


### [86] [Data-Efficient Deep Learning: Empirical Guidelines for Training Set Size Estimation in Inertial Sensor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09402)
*Ofir Kruzel,Itzik Klien*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了惯性分类任务中的学习曲线收敛特性，发现在六种真实数据集上准确率与样本量呈对数增长规律，并提出了稳定性点度量（stability point metric）来量化达到预期性能所需的最小样本量，从而为惯性传感应用中的数据采集提供了优化指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在惯性传感器分类任务（如人体活动识别、手机位置识别）中依赖大规模数据集，但数据采集耗时、复杂且难以扩展，且目前缺乏基于数据的准则来确定达到期望精度所需的最小样本量，因此需要系统性地研究学习曲线收敛规律以指导数据高效采集。

Method: 对二元和多类分类场景下的分类性能进行系统性实证评估，引入统一的分析框架；在六个真实世界数据集（总计102.7小时惯性测量）上测试，推导出经验公式用于估计性能与数据集大小的关系；提出稳定性点度量指标，定义为学习曲线稳定在预设平均绝对百分比偏差内的所需样本量。

Result: 准确率遵循一致的**对数增长模式**，与任务复杂度无关；模型通常在远少于传统启发式方法所建议的样本量下即可达到实际稳定性；从小规模预实验即可外推总数据需求。

Conclusion: 研究从最大化数据量范式转向优化数据效率范式，为惯性传感应用中的数据采集规划提供了具体的数据驱动指导原则，有助于平衡记录工作量与模型可靠性之间的关系。

Abstract: Deep learning models dependency on large-scale inertial datasets presents a significant bottleneck in inertial sensor-based classification tasks, such as human activity recognition and smartphone location recognition. In these domains, data collection requires massive recording campaigns that are complex, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. Currently, data-driven guidelines for determining the minimum sample size required to reach a desired accuracy level do not exist. To address this gap, this study presents a systematic empirical evaluation of learning curve convergence rates in inertial classification. We introduce a unified framework that analyzes classification performance under both binary and multi-class scenarios, and derive an empirical formula to estimate performance relative to dataset size. Testing across six diverse, real-world datasets totaling 102.7 hours of inertial measurements demonstrates that accuracy follows a consistent logarithmic growth pattern, regardless of task complexity. Leveraging this finding, we propose a quantitative stability point metric, defined as the sample size required for the learning curve to stabilize within a predefined mean absolute percentage deviation of its asymptotic maximum. Our analysis reveals that models often reach practical stability with substantially fewer samples than traditional heuristics suggest. Ultimately, we offer a generalizable framework to extrapolate total data requirements from small-scale pilot studies, optimizing the tradeoff between recording effort and model reliability. These findings shift the prevailing paradigm from maximizing data volume toward optimizing data efficiency, offering concrete, data-backed guidelines for planning recording campaigns in inertial sensing applications.

</details>


### [87] [SYNRARE: Synthetic Rare Disease EHR Generation for ML Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09404)
*Nicolai Dinh Khang Truong,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SYNRARE是一个基于Synthea框架的图形用户界面工具，用于生成与常见疾病患者仅有可控差异的罕见病患者的合成电子健康记录，从而帮助研究人员在受控技术条件下对机器学习算法进行基准测试和开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病诊断常因症状与常见疾病相似而延误，机器学习算法应用于电子健康记录有加速诊断的潜力，但法律和隐私问题构成重大障碍。现有合成数据生成算法缺乏支持生成与多数群体有可定义差异的患者子集（即模拟罕见病患者）的能力。

Method: 基于Synthea框架开发了名为SYNRARE的图形用户界面，支持对合成电子健康记录进行便捷修改和生成，使罕见病患者数据与常见疾病患者数据仅存在可定义程度的差异。

Result: SYNRARE使研究人员能够在受控技术条件下快速对其机器学习算法进行基准测试，适用于任何场景。

Conclusion: SYNRARE提供了一种解决隐私和法律问题的替代方案，通过生成可控差异的合成数据来支持罕见病诊断算法的开发和基准测试。

Abstract: Motivation: Rare disease (RD) diagnosis is frequently delayed due to the similarities in symptoms to common disease variants. Machine Learning Algorithms applied to Electronic Health Records show promise for accelerating the diagnosis; however, legal and privacy concerns pose significant barriers. To address these issues, Synthetic Data Generation is an alternative method for obtaining Electronic Health Records and can be applied with any Machine Learning algorithm for benchmarking and development purposes. Despite the availability of Synthetic Data Generation algorithms, support for generating a subset of patients that differ in a definable degree from the majority to simulate patients with RD is often lacking.
  Results: We present SYNRARE, a graphical user interface based on the Synthea framework that enables easier modification and generation of synthetic Electronic Health Records of RD patients, which differ only to a definable degree from patients with common diseases, thereby enabling the benchmarking and testing of algorithms under controlled technical conditions. SYNRARE enables researchers to rapidly benchmark their Machine Learning algorithms across any scenario.
  Availability and implementation: SYNRARE, including detailed instructions for installing, is available at https://gitlab.sdu.dk/screen4care/synrare.

</details>


### [88] [All Explanations are Wrong, But Many Are Useful: Exploring the Rashomon Explanation Set with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09502)
*Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rashomon Explanation范式与RashomonLLM方法，通过将解释与预测耦合，打破了可解释AI中常见的准确率-可解释性权衡，在多个任务中同时提升了预测准确率和解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI方法普遍存在准确率与可解释性之间的权衡问题，作者认为这一权衡并非根本性的，而是由于将解释和预测视为分离目标所致。当两者恰当耦合时，它们应当是互补而非互斥的。

Method: 提出Rashomon Explanation范式，构建一组忠实且引导预测的解释集合（而非单一解释），并证明该集合非空且解释保真度约束了其引导模型的性能上限。提出RashomonLLM，一种基于解释-预测-反思的智能体工作流，通过迭代对齐解释与预测来生成自然语言解释，并证明其收敛且能恢复完整解释集合。

Result: 在客户流失分类、临床生存回归和大规模直播日志点击率预测任务中，RashomonLLM在准确率和解释质量上均显著优于当前最先进的预测和可解释AI基线模型。其性能提升源自解释保真度，且在分布偏移、时间分割和随机种子下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该框架在提升商业性能的同时，为建立消费者信任奠定了理论基础，证明了准确率与可解释性可以兼得。

Abstract: Explaining machine-learning models is increasingly important for decision-making and consumer trust, yet it is widely believed to come at a cost: existing Explainable AI (XAI) methods suffer from a persistent accuracy-explainability trade-off. We argue that this trade-off is not fundamental, but an artifact of treating explanation and prediction as separate objectives; when properly coupled, they become complementary, so that equipping a model to explain itself improves, rather than degrades, its accuracy. We introduce the Rashomon Explanation paradigm, which builds a set of faithful, prediction-guiding explanations rather than a single one, and prove that this set is generally non-empty and that explanation fidelity bounds the performance of the models it guides. To explore this set, we propose RashomonLLM, an Explanation-Prediction-Reflection agentic workflow that generates explanations in natural language by iteratively aligning them with predictions, and we prove it converges and recovers the full set. Across customer-churn classification, clinical survival regression, and industrial click-through prediction on large-scale live-streaming logs, RashomonLLM significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prediction and XAI baselines on both accuracy and explanation quality, with gains driven by explanation fidelity and robust to distribution shifts, temporal splits, and seeds. Our framework thus advances business performance while laying the groundwork for consumer trust.

</details>


### [89] [Similarity search generalisation in contrastive learning with InfoNCE loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09405)
*Nick Whiteley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明InfoNCE损失函数在k个负样本下的总体风险以O(1/k)的速率逼近一个期望交叉熵，该交叉熵量化了学习嵌入函数在未见数据上的softmax相似度搜索与正样本生成器隐式表示的理想softmax搜索之间的偏差。此外，通过Gâteaux微分导出了InfoNCE损失的连续性界，该界保留了损失函数中对负样本取平均的结构，并包含一个可调"逆温度"参数。对于Lipschitz连续的嵌入函数，该界表明增加负样本数k可稳定泛化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释InfoNCE损失在有限负样本情况下的行为，补充已有关于k→∞极限的解释（互信息、对齐与均匀性），并为对比学习的泛化性能提供理论刻画。

Method: 理论分析：将InfoNCE的总体风险与期望交叉熵建立联系（O(1/k)逼近）；利用Gâteaux微分推导连续性泛化界。

Result: InfoNCE损失与期望交叉熵的偏差以O(1/k)速率衰减；导出的连续性界表明负样本数的增加可稳定泛化误差。

Conclusion: 有限负样本的InfoNCE损失可解释为近似理想softmax相似度搜索，且增加负样本数有助于提升泛化稳定性。

Abstract: Similarity search is a primary application of embedding models trained by contrastive learning. For one of the most popular contrastive learning loss functions, InfoNCE, we show that the population risk with $k$ negative samples is $O(1/k)$ close to an expected cross-entropy which quantifies deviation between i) a softmax similarity search over unseen data using the learned embedding function, and ii) an idealised softmax search over the same data but using similarity implicitly represented in the positive sample generator. This complements existing interpretations of InfoNCE in the $k\to\infty$ limit which are phrased in terms of mutual information, and alignment versus uniformity in embeddings. To quantify generalisation performance, we introduce a new continuity bound for the InfoNCE loss, obtained via Gâteaux differentiation. The bound preserves the structure of averaging over negative samples present in the loss function and features an ``inverse temperature'' parameter which can be tuned to account for the algorithmic temperature. For embedding functions which are Lipschitz in a parameter, this yields a simple demonstration that the averaging effect of $k$ negative samples in the InfoNCE loss carries over to stabilisation of the generalisation error as $k$ grows.

</details>


### [90] [Action-Factored Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Scalable Quantum Device Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09422)
*Edwin De Nicolo,Rahul Marchand,Cornelius Carlsson,Pranav Vaidhyanathan,Natalia Ares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QADAPT框架，通过在线学习动作空间的因子化表示来解耦多智能体，实现零样本泛化到未见过的量子设备尺寸，为大规模量子处理器快速校准提供可扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静电定义量子点阵列的调谐面临参数串扰导致的非平稳环境问题，这种效应同样困扰人工调谐，需要一种解耦多智能体以减少干扰的方法。

Method: 提出QADAPT框架，在线学习动作空间的因子化表示来解耦智能体并最小化干扰；基于局部测量和奖励高效学习共享策略。

Result: 实现零样本泛化到未见过的量子设备尺寸，且达到目标区域所需的收敛步数近似恒定。

Conclusion: 该模块化策略为大规模量子处理器的快速校准提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning is well suited to problems with large parameter spaces and exploitable local structure, such as the tuning of electrostatically-defined quantum-dot arrays. However, if parameter cross-talk is strong, a non-stationary environment from the perspective of any individual agent can destabilize learning - the same effect that plagues manual tuning of such systems. We propose using a factored representation of the action space, learned online, to decouple agents and minimize their interference. Our framework, QADAPT, uses this factorization to efficiently learn shared policies based on local measurements and rewards. With this modular strategy, we achieve zero-shot generalization to unseen quantum device sizes and maintain an approximately constant number of convergence steps to reach target regimes. This work provides a scalable route toward the rapid calibration of large-scale quantum processors.

</details>


### [91] [Active rejection enables reliable generalization of universal machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09456)
*Mingxiang Luo,Xinnan Mao,Lu Wang,Lei Bai,Feng Ding,Yuqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多教师路由（ATR）方法，仅用0.2%的真实r²SCAN标签即可为预训练生成289万个高质量伪标签，训练的轻量级CHGNet在多个基准和分子动力学模拟中显著优于基线模型，展示了主动拒绝机制将多个预训练uMLIP转化为可扩展可靠数据构建系统的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）虽能桥接量子力学精度与大尺度分子动力学，但高精度计算（如r²SCAN）成本高昂导致训练数据集相对于开放材料空间仍然很小，且平均基准性能优秀并不能保证对每个结构都有可靠的能量-力预测。

Method: 将高保真数据构建重构为结构层面的不确定性决策问题：用少量真实r²SCAN标签校准多个预训练uMLIP教师，结合结构描述符、教师身份和教师间分歧来估计每个结构-教师对的可靠性，选择高置信度预测生成伪标签，拒绝无足够可靠教师的结构。

Result: 仅用0.2%候选结构的真实r²SCAN标签，生成了289万个可追溯的r²SCAN级别伪标签用于预训练；在留出测试集和MP-r²SCAN基准上，ATR训练的CHGNet持续优于基线和无路由的对照组；有限温度分子动力学显示ATR在多材料系统中提升了动力学鲁棒性，维持了稳定轨迹而基线模拟发生灾难性结构崩塌。

Conclusion: 主动拒绝机制是将多个预训练uMLIP转化为可扩展且可靠的高保真数据构建系统的有效方法。

Abstract: Universal machine learning interatomic potentials (uMLIPs) bridge quantum-mechanical accuracy and large-scale molecular dynamics, but the cost of high-accuracy calculations such as r$^2$SCAN limits training to datasets that remain small relative to the open materials space. Strong average benchmark performance also does not guarantee reliable energy--force predictions for every structure. We propose Adaptive Multi-Teacher Routing (ATR), which reformulates high-fidelity data construction as a structure-wise decision problem under uncertainty. Using a small set of real r$^2$SCAN labels, ATR calibrates multiple pretrained uMLIP teachers and combines structural descriptors, teacher identity, and inter-teacher disagreement to estimate the reliability of each structure--teacher pair. It selects high-confidence predictions for pseudo-label generation and rejects structures for which no teacher is sufficiently reliable. With real r$^2$SCAN labels for only 0.2\% of candidate structures, ATR distils 2.89 million traceable r$^2$SCAN-level pseudo-labels for pretraining. On held-out r$^2$SCAN structures and the MP-r$^2$SCAN benchmark, a lightweight CHGNet trained on the ATR-generated dataset consistently outperforms the baseline and non-routed controls. Finite-temperature molecular dynamics further shows that ATR improves dynamical robustness across multiple material systems, maintaining stable trajectories where baseline simulations undergo catastrophic structural collapse. These results establish active rejection as an effective mechanism for converting multiple pretrained uMLIPs into a scalable and reliable data-construction system for high-fidelity uMLIPs.

</details>


### [92] [Semantic Pareto-DQN: A Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Framework for Financial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09641)
*Cláudio Lúcio do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语义帕累托-DQN，一种多目标强化学习框架，通过大语言模型将异构交易特征编码为自然语言叙事，构建鲁棒的尺度不变状态表示，并优化解耦财务效能、操作摩擦与语义发现的向量化奖励，在类别极度不平衡的金融异常检测中打破零召回陷阱，实现更优的少数类召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统金融异常检测算法因极端类别不平衡而出现"欺诈塌缩"现象（默认预测为多数类），且单目标框架无法在异常拦截与客户摩擦之间取得平衡，而现有数据重采样方法具有失真问题。

Method: 提出语义帕累托-DQN多目标强化学习框架：1）利用大语言模型将异构交易特征合成为连贯的自然语言叙事，生成鲁棒的尺度不变状态表示；2）优化向量化奖励，解耦财务效能、操作摩擦和语义发现三个目标；3）通过连续帕累托前沿映射，动态导航遗漏异常与误报之间的不对称成本。

Result: 在电商欺诈和UCI信用数据集上，语义帕累托-DQN成功打破了零召回陷阱，相比标量化基线方法取得了更优的少数类召回率，证明了在可控操作摩擦代价下提升金融异常发现能力的可行性。

Conclusion: 语义帕累托-DQN为金融异常检测中极端类别不平衡问题提供了无需数据重采样的有效替代方案，通过多目标强化学习框架实现了异常拦截与客户摩擦之间的帕累托最优权衡。

Abstract: Financial anomaly detection suffers from extreme class imbalance, causing traditional single-objective algorithms to exhibit ``fraud collapse'', defaulting to the majority class and failing to balance anomaly interdiction with customer friction. To overcome this without distortive data resampling, we propose the Semantic Pareto-DQN, a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework. Our approach synthesizes heterogeneous transaction features into cohesive natural-language narratives, encoded by large language models, thereby producing a robust, scale-invariant state representation. The agent optimizes a vectorial reward that explicitly decouples financial efficacy, operational friction, and semantic discovery. By mapping the continuous Pareto frontier, the system dynamically navigates the asymmetric costs of missed anomalies versus false positives. Empirical evaluations across E-Commerce fraud and UCI Credit datasets show that semantic Pareto-DQN successfully shatters the zero-recall trap. It achieves superior minority-class recall compared to scalarized baselines, providing an alternative to trade bounded operational friction for financial anomaly discovery.

</details>


### [93] [TSAI-MetaFraud: A Benchmark Dataset for Financial Fraud Transaction and Behavioral Risk Detection in Metaverse Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09528)
*Refat Ishrak Hemel,Ehsan Hallaji,Roozbeh Razavi-Far*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSAI-MetaFraud是一个面向虚拟经济欺诈分析的多模态、多任务基准数据集，整合了行为、交易和图结构信息，包含欺诈和机器人场景，支持交易欺诈检测、跨模态节点分类、时序链接预测和弱监督欺诈检测等任务，并提供了机器学习和图神经网络的基线评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集通常孤立地关注用户行为、认证或金融交易中的单一维度，限制了多模态欺诈检测方法的开发和可重复评估，亟需一个统一的多模态基准数据集来推动虚拟经济中的可信分析。

Method: 构建了TSAI-MetaFraud多模态基准数据集，整合行为、交易和图结构三种信息模态，设计四种基准任务（交易欺诈检测、跨模态节点分类、时序链接预测、弱监督欺诈检测），并使用机器学习模型和图神经网络进行基线评估。

Result: 提供了一个统一的虚拟经济多模态基准数据集，并给出了多种模型在四个欺诈分析任务上的基线评估结果。

Conclusion: TSAI-MetaFraud为元宇宙生态系统中的多模态学习、图挖掘、欺诈分析和可信AI研究提供了标准化基准，有助于推动相关领域的可重复研究。

Abstract: The emergence of metaverse platforms has created virtual economies that introduce new challenges related to fraud, bot activity, and illicit financial behavior. Despite growing interest in trustworthy metaverse analytics, existing datasets typically focus on user behavior, authentication, or financial transactions in isolation, limiting the development and reproducible evaluation of multimodal fraud detection methods. To address this gap, we present TSAI-MetaFraud, a multimodal, multi-task benchmark dataset for fraud analytics in virtual economies. TSAI-MetaFraud integrates behavioral, transactional, and graph-structured information while incorporating realistic fraud and automated bot scenarios. We define benchmark tasks including transaction fraud detection, cross-modal node classification, temporal link prediction, and weakly supervised fraud detection, and provide baseline evaluations using machine learning models and graph neural networks. By jointly capturing behavioral activity, financial interactions, and relational structure within a unified virtual economy, TSAI-MetaFraud provides a benchmark for advancing multimodal learning, graph mining, fraud analytics, and trustworthy AI in emerging metaverse ecosystems.

</details>


### [94] [Statistically Undetectable Backdoors in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09532)
*Andrej Bogdanov,Alon Rosen,Neekon Vafa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在深度前馈神经网络中，攻击者可以植入在统计上不可检测的后门（即使在白盒设置中，后门模型与诚实训练模型的总变差距离仍很小），该后门可为每个输入提供基于不变性的对抗样本，使相距很远的输入映射到异常接近的输出；而在没有后门时，在标准密码学假设下无法在多项式时间内生成任何此类对抗样本。理论和初步实验表明，模型训练者与模型用户之间存在根本性的能力不对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示模型训练者与模型用户之间根本性的能力不对称——训练者可以植入统计上不可检测的后门，而用户在没有后门的情况下无法在多项式时间内生成对抗样本，从而指出当前机器学习安全范式中存在被忽视的威胁。

Method: 利用密码学工具和统计不可区分性理论，设计了一种在白盒设置下仍能保持总变差距离极小的后门植入方法；后门通过构造特定的权重分布，使模型对输入空间中的某些隐式不变性敏感，从而产生不当的输入-输出映射关系。

Result: 后门模型与诚实模型在统计上不可区分（白盒）；后门提供对每个输入都有效的基于不变性的对抗样本；在没有后门时，标准密码学假设下多项式时间内无法生成任何此类对抗样本。

Conclusion: 模型训练者拥有不对称的对抗能力，能够在保持统计不可检测性的前提下植入后门，这给现有的模型审计和认证方式带来了根本性挑战；需要重新审视训练者与用户之间的信任模型。

Abstract: We show how an adversarial model trainer can plant backdoors in a large class of deep, feedforward neural networks. These backdoors are statistically undetectable in the white-box setting, meaning that the backdoored and honestly trained models are close in total variation distance, even given the full descriptions of the models (e.g., all of the weights). The backdoor provides access to invariance-based adversarial examples for every input, mapping distant inputs to unusually close outputs. However, without the backdoor, it is provably impossible (under standard cryptographic assumptions) to generate any such adversarial examples in polynomial time. Our theoretical and preliminary empirical findings demonstrate a fundamental power asymmetry between model trainers and model users.

</details>


### [95] [GatedLinear: Adaptive Routing of Complementary Linear Bases for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09537)
*Qitai Tan,Ruiwen Gu,Yilin Su,Mo Li,Xu Lin,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GatedLinear框架，通过三因子融合门控自适应路由三种互补线性基（全局趋势-季节、差分增量、相位对齐循环），实现轻量级高精度时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度时序模型使用单一计算骨干（如自注意力或谱滤波）处理多样的、互斥的时序动态（趋势、非平稳漂移、相位对齐周期），难以应对真实序列的异质性。

Method: 构建三个专用线性机制池（全局趋势-季节基、差分增量基、相位对齐循环基），设计三因子分解融合门（Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate）将路由决策分解为通道偏好、视界偏移和相位偏置，实现逐点软路由。

Result: 在标准基准上达到与复杂基础模型相当或更优的SOTA精度，参数规模显著更小，且路由模式具有明确可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级线性基础的自适应路由替代单机制重型神经网络，兼顾了预测精度、可解释性和计算效率。

Abstract: Time series forecasting requires models to capture diverse, often mutually exclusive, temporal dynamics, from smooth trend continuation to nonstationary drift and strict phase-aligned recurrence. While recent deep learning models have improved accuracy, they typically force these diverse patterns through a single computational backbone governed by fixed algorithmic inductive biases (e.g., self-attention or spectral filtering). This single-mechanism approach often struggles with the profound heterogeneity of real-world series, where different variables and forecast horizons necessitate fundamentally different predictive treatments. To address this, we propose GatedLinear: a lightweight framework that frames forecasting as the adaptive routing of complementary linear bases. GatedLinear leverages a pool of three specialized mechanisms: a global trend-seasonal basis for smooth projection, a difference-based incremental basis for nonstationary drift, and a phase-aligned recurrence basis for explicit cyclic reuse. To dynamically orchestrate these distinct behaviors, we introduce a Tri-Factorized Fusion Gate that disentangles routing decisions into channel-specific preferences, horizon-aware offsets, and phase-indexed biases derived from known future time marks. This design allows the model to perform highly granular, point-wise soft routing across different predictive regimes without stacking computationally heavy neural modules. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that our method achieves state-of-the-art or highly competitive accuracy against recent complex foundational models, while offering explicitly interpretable routing patterns and operating with a substantially smaller parameter footprint.

</details>


### [96] [CoCoT-EEG: Contrastive-Pretrained Multiscale Convolutional Transformer for EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09543)
*Gabriel Mahuas,Victoria Shevchenko,Ugo Tanielian,Yassir Bendou,Richard Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoT模型，采用对比预训练策略替代传统的掩码重建预训练，结合多尺度时间卷积输入层和Transformer编码器，在EEG解码任务上达到或超越现有最先进模型，且从头训练即可媲美预训练模型，展示了对比学习在脑电基础模型构建中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于掩码重建预训练的EEG基础模型因数据噪声大、信息集中在有限维度（如窄频带）而表现次优，亟需探索更有效的预训练策略。

Method: 提出CoCoT（Contrastive-pretrained CoT），采用对比预训练范式，结合多尺度时间卷积输入层提取层次化特征，并引入Transformer编码器进行序列建模。

Result: 在多种异构电极配置的EEG基准解码任务上，CoCoT匹配或超越基于重建预训练的最先进模型；从头训练的CoCoT超越先前单任务解码模型，甚至媲美预训练模型。

Conclusion: 对比学习在构建EEG基础模型中具有可行性，同时揭示了关键架构设计考量，为替代性大规模预训练策略提供了新方向。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretrained foundation models (FM) have shown early promise for non-invasive electroencephalogram (EEG) decoding applications. Many recent large-scale models converged on the approach of tokenizing raw EEG followed by masked reconstruction pretraining. However, this recipe has been shown to be suboptimal for data, like EEG, with high noise amplitude and information confined to limited dimensions such as narrow frequency bands. Building on this insight, we develop a novel contrastive-pretrained EEG model with multiscale temporal convolution input layers and Transformer encoder blocks (CoCoT). CoCoT matches or beats state-of-the-art reconstruction-pretrained EEG models on extensive benchmark decoding tasks with heterogeneous electrode configurations. Furthermore, CoCoT trained from scratch outperforms previous single-task decoding models and even rivals pretrained models, showcasing the architecture's flexibility and data efficiency. Through systematic ablations, including model architecture and pretraining objective, we demonstrate the viability of contrastive learning for building EEG FMs while suggesting key architectural design considerations, prompting further investigations in alternative large-scale pretraining strategies.

</details>


### [97] [Graph-Regularized Low-Rank Matrix Completion by Variable Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09546)
*Benoît Loucheur,P. -A. Absil,Michel Journée*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GR-RTRMC方法，在图正则化框架下结合黎曼信赖域优化，利用矩阵行列间的内在关系来提升低秩矩阵补全的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RTRMC方法仅利用低秩约束的几何结构，未充分利用矩阵行列间的关联信息，导致在数据存在强相关性时补全效果不佳。

Method: 将图正则化项融入RTRMC框架，在Grassmann流形上将原问题重新建模为无约束优化问题，通过捕获行列间的关系来约束低秩矩阵补全过程。

Result: 在数据行列存在强相关性的场景下，GR-RTRMC相比原RTRMC方法在补全准确性和鲁棒性上取得提升。

Conclusion: 图正则化能有效利用矩阵的结构化先验信息，增强低秩矩阵补全方法在相关数据上的表现。

Abstract: We address the low-rank matrix completion problem by incorporating graph regularization into the existing Riemannian Trust-Region Matrix Completion (RTRMC) framework. The latter uses the geometry of the low-rank constraint to remodel the problem as an unconstrained optimization problem on a single Grassmann manifold. Our approach, named Graph-Regularized RTRMC (GR-RTRMC), exploits the inherent relationships between rows and columns of the matrix. By using these relationships, we aim to improve the accuracy and robustness of matrix completion, particularly in scenarios where the underlying data exhibits strong correlations between rows or columns.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [STEEL: Sparsity-Aware Fused Attention for Energy-Efficient Long-Sequence Inference on AMD's XDNA NPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09385)
*Victor J. B. Jung,Gagandeep Singh,Joseph Melber,Kristof Denolf,Francesco Conti,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: STEEL是首个面向XDNA类NPU的开源FlashAttention实现，通过数据流预填充注意力公式和稀疏感知流水线放置，在AMD Ryzen AI 9 HX 370 SoC上相比CPU和GPU分别实现9.17倍和1.75倍的能耗降低，相比先前最优方案实现9.6倍延迟缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在笔记本电脑SoC上的部署日益普及，但云端卸载存在可靠性和隐私问题，而NPU的注意力机制映射面临架构多样性和显式数据移动编程模型的挑战，亟需高效的片上注意力计算方案。

Method: 提出STEEL框架：1) 设计面向XDNA类NPU的数据流预填充注意力公式，利用空间并行性和片上存储器；2) 采用稀疏感知的流水线放置策略，通过关注因果掩码导致的负载不均衡来减少同步开销并提升NPU阵列利用率。

Result: 在AMD Ryzen AI 9 HX 370 SoC上，相比CPU和GPU基线，能耗分别降低9.17倍和1.75倍。在XDNA 1上相比先前最优方案延迟降低9.6倍，在XDNA 2上相比逐层注意力实现加速22.8倍。

Conclusion: STEEL通过有效利用NPU架构特性，在保持低能耗的同时显著提升注意力机制推理性能，为边缘端LLM智能体部署提供了高效可行的片上计算方案。

Abstract: The growing adoption of large language model-based agents within operating system workflows has increased the importance of energy-efficient inference on laptop-class systems-on-chip (SoCs). While cloud offloading remains common, it introduces reliability and privacy concerns that are particularly problematic for agentic workloads. Recent laptop SoCs, therefore, incorporate neural processing engines (NPUs) optimized for energy efficiency; however, effectively mapping attention mechanisms onto NPUs remains challenging due to architectural diversity and explicit data-movement programming models. In this work, we present STEEL, the first open-source implementation of FlashAttention targeting XDNA-like NPUs. STEEL introduces a dataflow formulation of prefill attention, enabling efficient exploitation of spatial parallelism and on-chip memory. Furthermore, STEEL addresses the load imbalance induced by the causal mask by leveraging a sparsity-aware pipeline placement onto the NPU array, reducing synchronization overhead and improving utilization. We evaluate STEEL on the AMD Ryzen AI 9 HX 370 SoC and compare its performance against optimized CPU and GPU implementations. Experimental results show that STEEL reduces energy consumption by an average of 9.17x and 1.75x relative to CPU and GPU baselines, respectively. On XDNA 1, STEEL achieves an average 9.6x latency reduction over the prior state of the art, and delivers a 22.8x speedup on average compared to a layer-by-layer attention implementation on XDNA 2.

</details>


### [99] [SiFAR: Synchronization-Free All-Reduce for Low-Latency LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08973)
*Hritvik Taneja,Anish Saxena,Abhishek Revinipati,Jae Hyung Ju,Neal C. Crago,Moinuddin Qureshi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对推理模型和智能体系统中LLM令牌生成延迟的瓶颈问题，本文提出SiFAR（无同步全规约）方法，通过消除All-Reduce中的同步开销，在TP=8时将全规约延迟降低52%，端到端吞吐量提升18.6%（Llama-3.1-8B）和13.1%（Qwen3.5-397B-17B）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型和智能体系统生成大量中间推理令牌，这些令牌不由人类消费，因此每令牌延迟直接决定端到端响应时间。低延迟推理采用最小批处理，使令牌生成受带宽限制。张量并行通过跨GPU分散模型权重来缓解该问题，但扩展到更多GPU时，All-Reduce的同步开销随GPU数量增长，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出SiFAR框架，包含三项关键创新：(1) 消除one-shot算法中的底部屏障（WAW依赖），通过协同设计通信与模型执行实现双缓冲；(2) 利用现代交换机的中继归约能力，提出冗余拉取（redundant pull），使每个GPU在交换机处归约完整全规约负载，提升one-shot的可扩展性；(3) 提出推测性归约（speculative reduction），在顶部屏障前发起数据传输，通过轻量级验证确保正确性，减少顶部屏障开销。

Result: SiFAR将All-Reduce延迟降低52%，端到端吞吐量提升：Llama-3.1-8B提升18.6%，Qwen3.5-397B-17B提升13.1%（TP=8时）。去除All-Reduce可使Llama-3.1-8B在8块H200 GPU上的令牌吞吐量提升43%。

Conclusion: SiFAR通过消除同步屏障开销显著降低了低延迟推理场景下的All-Reduce延迟，在保持正确性的前提下提升了张量并行的可扩展性和端到端吞吐量，适用于现代推理模型和智能体系统。

Abstract: The rise of reasoning models and agentic systems has made LLM token-generation latency a key bottleneck. Unlike chatbots, whose latency gains saturate at human reading speed, these systems generate intermediate reasoning tokens not consumed by humans. Thus, per-token latency directly determines end-to-end response time. Low-latency inference uses minimal batching, making token generation bandwidth-bound. Tensor Parallelism addresses this by sharding model weights across GPUs and loading them in parallel. However, scaling to more GPUs introduces All-Reduce overheads that grow with GPU count. Removing All-Reduce improves token throughput by 43% for Llama-3.1-8B on 8 H200 GPUs.
  We propose Synchronization-Free All-Reduce (SiFAR), which reduces synchronization overhead during low-latency inference. Existing oneshot and twoshot algorithms incur overheads from barriers before and after communication. First, we find that the bottom barrier in oneshot enforces a WAW dependency and eliminate it by co-designing communication and model execution to enable dual buffering. However, oneshot scales poorly with GPU count. Twoshot performs better at higher TP degrees but incurs an unavoidable bottom barrier. To overcome this, we leverage in-switch reduction in modern switches. We propose redundant pull, where each GPU reduces the full All-Reduce payload at the switch. This improves oneshot scalability while retaining its no-bottom-barrier advantage. Finally, to reduce top-barrier overhead, we observe that each decode step issues multiple All-Reduce operations, keeping GPUs tightly synchronized after the first. We therefore propose speculative reduction, which initiates data transfer before the top barrier and ensures correctness via lightweight validation. SiFAR reduces All-Reduce latency by up to 52% and improves end-to-end throughput by 18.6% for Llama-3.1-8B and 13.1% for Qwen3.5-397B-17B at TP=8.

</details>


### [100] [Distributed Symmetry Breaking on Hyperbolic Random Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09170)
*Yannic Maus,Janosch Ruff,Sonia Simons,George Skretas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲随机图（HRG）上的分布式算法。虽然HRG上的Δ+1着色问题仅需2轮即可解决，但本文证明最大独立集（MIS）和最大匹配（MM）问题在HRG上存在Ω(log log n / log log log n)的下界，并设计了接近最优的算法达到Õ(log^{5/3} log n)轮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到许多算法在真实世界网络上的表现远超最坏情况理论保证，因此希望在更符合真实网络特性的双曲随机图模型上分析分布式算法的真实复杂度。

Method: 通过揭示HRG中包含具有大高度和大度数的d-叉树这一结构性洞察，将已有的不可能性结果适配并提升到HRG场景，从而建立下界；同时设计了针对HRG特性的专用分布式算法来实现上界。

Result: 证明了MIS和MM在HRG上的下界为Ω(log log n / log log log n)轮，并设计了Õ(log^{5/3} log n)轮的算法，该上界多项式紧于下界，且优于通用的Ω(min{log Δ, √log n})轮最坏情况界。

Conclusion: 在HRG模型中，MIS和MM本质上比Δ+1着色更难，存在显著的对数对数级下界，但分布式算法仍能获得优于通用图的性能。

Abstract: Real-world networks like the internet share patterns like a power law degree distribution and a high clustering coefficient. Many of these properties are captured by the generative model of hyperbolic random graphs (HRGs), which provides a theoretical framework for studying such networks. Motivated by the observation that several algorithms perform better on real-world networks than their worst-case guarantees suggest, we design and analyse distributed algorithms under the assumption that the input graph is an HRG. Indeed, prior work has shown that the classical symmetry-breaking problem of $Δ+1$ colouring, where $Δ$ is the maximum degree of the graph, can be solved in 2 rounds on HRGs [Maus and Ruff; SODA'26].
  In stark contrast to this 2-round algorithm for $Δ+1$ colouring, we prove that the related symmetry-breaking problems of maximal independent set (MIS) and maximal matching (MM) are substantially harder: we establish a lower bound of $Ω\left(\frac{\log\log n}{\log\log\log n}\right)$ for MIS and MM on HRGs. Our lower bound techniques rely on new structural insights that may be of independent interest: we show that HRGs contain $d$-ary trees with large height and degree which enables us to adapt and lift prior impossibility results for distributed algorithms to the setting of HRGs.
  We also show that these lower bounds are polynomial tight: we design algorithms tailored to HRGs that solve MIS and MM in $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(\log^{5/3}\log n)$ rounds with high probability in the LOCAL model, improving over the general worst-case lower bound of $Ω\left(\min\left\{\log Δ, \sqrt{\log n}\right\}\right)$ rounds [Khoury and Schild; FOCS'25].

</details>


### [101] [Bidirectional Resource Scheduling for Disaggregated and Asynchronous RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09207)
*Tan Zhiqiang,Wang Maoxin,Wang Sijie,Yin Yiming,Wang Qiang,Chu Xiaowen,Shi Shaohuai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出BiDiRL，一种用于异步、分离式强化学习RL的混合时空复用架构，通过热切换运行时、静态规划器和双向调度器减少资源空闲，在32-GPU测试床上将RL训练吞吐量提升高达1.94倍，且不影响收敛行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能RL框架（如StreamRL、AReaL）采用分离式架构和异步rollout，但在不同RL设置（硬件配置、模型规模、陈旧度、超参数）和变化的工作负载下，rollout资源和训练资源仍经常出现空闲期，导致资源利用率不足。

Method: BiDiRL包含三个关键组件：1) 热切换运行时（hot-switch runtime），实现rollout与训练资源间的快速切换，开销可忽略；2) 基于时间性能建模的静态调度感知规划器，选择利于热切换的资源分区，使rollout和训练时长在粗粒度上大致平衡；3) 双向调度器，在运行时通过细粒度资源切换，允许瓶颈阶段临时从另一资源池借用空闲资源，利用运行时气泡。

Result: 在两个32-GPU测试床上，跨多种工作负载、数据集和模型，BiDiRL相比veRL、AReaL、ROLL等RL系统，RL训练吞吐量提升高达1.94倍，且不影响收敛行为。

Conclusion: BiDiRL通过混合时空复用架构有效解决了异步分离式RL中资源空闲问题，在不牺牲收敛性能的前提下显著提升了系统吞吐量，验证了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: It is well established that the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) can be improved by applying reinforcement learning (RL) in a post-training stage. In a standard RL iteration, the current model (the policy) generates experience through rollouts, and the resulting data is then used to update the policy during training. High-performance RL frameworks such as StreamRL and AReaL employ a disaggregated architecture and asynchronous rollouts to better exploit both rollout and training resources, thereby increasing overall system throughput.
  Nonetheless, across varying RL setups (e.g., hardware configurations, model scales, staleness levels, and hyperparameters) and under changing workloads, it remains common for both rollout and training resources to experience idle periods.
  In this paper, we present BiDiRL, a hybrid time-space multiplexing architecture for asynchronous, disaggregated RL designed to reduce resource idleness. First, we develop a hot-switch runtime that enables rapid switching between rollout and training resources with negligible overhead. Second, we propose a static, scheduling-aware planner based on time-performance modeling that chooses a hot-switch-friendly resource partition, so that rollout and training durations are roughly balanced at a coarse level. Third, at execution time, we introduce a bidirectional scheduler that further exploits runtime bubbles through fine-grained resource switching, allowing the bottleneck stage to temporarily borrow idle resources from the other pool. Across a wide range of workloads, datasets, and models on two 32-GPU testbeds, BiDiRL increases RL training throughput by up to 1.94x compared with RL systems including veRL, AReaL, and ROLL, without affecting convergence behavior.

</details>


### [102] [EcoKube: Simulating Carbon-Aware Scheduling Policies in Heterogeneous Edge-Cloud Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09318)
*Gonçalo Ferreira,Shashikant Ilager*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EcoKube是一个可配置的仿真框架，用于在异构边缘-云环境中可重复评估可持续性感知调度策略，支持事件驱动模拟、策略接口和异构感知参考策略，并通过合成批处理负载对多种调度策略进行了比较验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有碳感知工作虽探索了时间弹性、时空负载迁移等方案，但缺乏一个一致且可重复的工作流来评估分布式异构边缘-云拓扑上的可持续性感知调度策略。

Method: 构建EcoKube仿真框架，包含事件驱动确定性模拟器、策略钩子接口和异构感知参考策略；通过合成批处理负载，对比默认Kubernetes调度器、KEIDS和TOPSIS/KCSS等策略进行实验评估。

Result: EcoKube框架能够可重复地比较多种可持续性感知调度策略在异构边缘-云环境下的性能表现。

Conclusion: EcoKube提供了架构和实验两方面的贡献，为部署前比较可持续性感知策略提供了可复现的评估方法。

Abstract: Energy demand from cloud and edge computing is rising rapidly, with AI workloads further intensifying electricity use and associated carbon emissions. In hybrid edge-cloud settings, sustainability impact depends on time- and location-varying grid Carbon Intensity (CI), site Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE), and heterogeneous hardware characteristics. Existing carbon-aware work explores solutions such as temporal elasticity, spatio-temporal workload shifting, and carbon-aware placement across distributed sites. However, these solutions do not provide a consistent and reproducible workflow for evaluating sustainability-aware scheduling policies on heterogeneous, federated edge-cloud topologies. We present EcoKube: a configurable simulation framework for the reproducible evaluation of sustainability-aware scheduling policies in heterogeneous edge-cloud environments. The framework includes an event-driven deterministic simulator, policy hooks, and a heterogeneity-aware reference policy. We evaluate the framework with synthetic batch workloads, comparing the reference policy against the default Kubernetes scheduler, KEIDS, and TOPSIS/KCSS. The contribution is architectural and experimental: EcoKube provides a reproducible way to compare sustainability-aware policies before deployment.

</details>


### [103] [Short Graph Sketches Suffice for Error-resilient Leader Verification in CONGEST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09522)
*Pawel Garncarek,Tomasz Jurdziński,Dariusz Kowalski,Subhajit Pramanick*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了CONGEST模型下的局部可检验带错误证明（LCPE）。针对树上的唯一领导者验证问题，提出了局部图草图、想象树和想象认证等概念，设计出仅需O(ε² log n)位通信、O(ε²)轮的LCPE算法，可容忍ε个对抗性证书错误，并证明了匹配的不可行性结果：视图距离不超过ε的方案无法容忍ε个证书错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LCPE算法基于LOCAL模型设计，其无限通信能力使其无法直接应用于带宽受限的CONGEST模型。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究CONGEST模型下高效的LCPE实现。

Method: 引入局部图草图（local graph sketches）、想象树（imagined trees）和想象认证（imagined certifications）概念，将每个节点所需的(2ε+1)跳邻域信息压缩编码为仅O(ε² log n)位，并设计O(ε²)轮CONGEST通信算法来构建这些草图。

Result: 提出了首个CONGEST模型下的LCPE算法，可容忍ε个对抗性证书错误；同时证明了下界：即使在LOCAL模型且证书大小无限制下，视图距离不超过ε的方案也无法容忍ε个错误，表明视图距离必须超过ε。

Conclusion: 本文建立了CONGEST模型中LCPE的可行性边界：视图距离与错误容忍度之间存在根本性权衡，为带宽受限分布式系统中的容错证明提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Locally Checkable Proofs (LCPs) enable the verification of global graph properties using locally checkable certificates assigned by a prover. Recently, this framework was extended to Locally Checkable Proofs-with-Errors (LCPE), where an adversary may corrupt some certificates. Existing LCPE algorithms, however, are designed for the LOCAL model, whose unbounded communication makes them unsuitable for direct implementation in the bandwidth-restricted CONGEST model.
  We initiate the study of efficient CONGEST implementations of LCPE through the \textsc{unique-leader} verification problem on trees. The main challenge is that tolerating $\varepsilon$ certificate errors requires each node to reason about its $(2\varepsilon+1)$-hop neighborhood, whose exact topology may require up to $O(Δ^{2\varepsilon+1}\log n)$ bits to communicate. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce \emph{local graph sketches}, together with the notions of \emph{imagined trees} and \emph{imagined certifications}, which encode precisely the information needed for verification using only $O(\varepsilon^2\log n)$ bits per node. Based on these sketches, we design an LCPE algorithm that tolerates up to $\varepsilon$ adversarial certificate errors and constructs the required sketches in $O(\varepsilon^2)$ communication rounds in the CONGEST model.
  We complement our algorithm with a matching impossibility result: even in the strictly more powerful LOCAL model, and even with unbounded certificate size, no verification scheme with view distance at most $\varepsilon$ can tolerate $\varepsilon$ adversarial certificate errors. Since every CONGEST algorithm can be simulated in LOCAL, this lower bound immediately applies to CONGEST, showing that a view distance exceeding $\varepsilon$ is unavoidable.

</details>


### [104] [New Complexity Classes in Locally Checkable Labeling for Local Computation Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09626)
*Sijin Peng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究局部计算算法（LCA）的复杂度分类，聚焦于局部可检查标记（LCL）问题。通过在VOLUME模型和LCA模型中堆叠已知复杂度的实例，构造出探测复杂度为Θ(log^k n)和Θ̃(n^{p/q})的LCL问题（k≥1, p/q∈(0,1]）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究给定复杂度函数f(n)是否存在对应的LCA问题（即LCA的复杂度分类），从而理解不同复杂度层次是否存在自然的问题实例。

Method: 将Rosenbaum和Suomela (2020)在VOLUME模型中构造的复杂度为Θ(log n)和Θ̃(n^{1/k})的LCL实例进行堆叠（stacking），得到新的复杂度层次，并推广到LCA模型。

Result: 构造了探测复杂度为Θ(log^k n)（任意正整数k≥1）和Θ̃(n^{p/q})（任意有理数p/q∈(0,1]）的LCL问题，涵盖了从对数到多项式范围的连续复杂度谱。

Conclusion: VOLUME/LCA模型中LCL问题的复杂度可以取对数多项式及有理数幂次的多项式值，且构造方法不同于分布式LOCAL模型中Balliu等人的方法。

Abstract: Local Computation Algorithms (LCAs), introduced by Rubinfeld, Tamir, Vardi, and Xie (2011), are a special type of sublinear algorithms that, given probing access to a possibly massive input, are required to provide query access to a consistent solution, without maintaining a state between different queries. In this paper, we try to understand LCA through the lens of complexity classifications, described by the following question: Given a target complexity function $f(n)$, is there a problem whose local computation complexity is $f(n)$, up to polylogarithmic factors?
  We restrict our focus to Locally Checkable Labeling (LCL) problems, which can be seen as constant-degree constraint satisfaction problems. Possible complexity classes of this problem family have been extensively studied in various distributed computation models, including the $\mathrm{VOLUME}$ model proposed by Rosenbaum and Suomela (2020), which is an invariant of local computation algorithms with additional locality requirements.
  In this paper, we provide new LCL complexity constructions in the $\mathrm{VOLUME}$ model, and generalize the results to LCAs. Specifically, we show that there are LCLs whose probe complexities in the $\mathrm{VOLUME}$ and LCA models are $Θ(\log^k n)$ and $\tilde Θ(n^{p/q})$ for any positive integer $k \ge 1$ and rational $p/q \in (0,1]$. Our approach, completely different from the approach to a similar result in the distributed $\mathrm{LOCAL}$ model by Balliu et al. (2018), is to stack instances of complexity $Θ(\log n)$ and $\tilde Θ(n^{1/k})$ in the $\mathrm{VOLUME}$ model constructed by Rosenbaum and Suomela (2020).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Interval Certifications for Multilayered Perceptrons via Lattice Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08773)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个严格的理論框架，将AI安全中的对抗鲁棒性问题归约为格点遍历问题，并区分了“声音认证”（区间内预测不变）和“完整认证”（区间外预测必变）两种认证类型。文章开发了格遍历算子，采用“细化-验证”迭代方案，利用形式化MLP验证器保证声音最大性和完整最小性。在优化问题中发现非对称性：完整认证可在多项式时间内求解，而声音认证是强难解的。此外，针对对称区间（ℓ∞球）提出了对数时间算法，并通过ParallelepipedoNN系统进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全中的对抗鲁棒性是基础性问题，现有工作主要关注声音认证（区间内预测不变），但对完整认证（区间外预测必变）缺乏研究。本文旨在建立统一的理论框架，揭示两类认证的数学结构及其在计算复杂性上的根本差异。

Method: 将对抗鲁棒性问题归约为格点遍历问题，每个格元素对应包含输入点的轴对齐超矩形区间。开发格遍历算子，采用“细化-验证”迭代方案，结合形式化MLP验证器实现声音最大性和完整最小性保证。通过计算复杂性理论分析优化问题的可解性，并针对对称区间设计对数算法。

Result: 完整认证的最小解可在多项式时间内（多项式次oracle调用）求得；声音认证的优化问题是强难解的（strong intractability）。对称区间（ℓ∞球）上可实现对数时间算法。通过ParallelepipedoNN系统进行了实证验证。

Conclusion: 对抗鲁棒性问题可严格归约为格遍历问题；声音认证与完整认证在计算复杂性上存在根本性不对称；对称区间上的优化问题具有高效算法。

Abstract: In this work we present a rigorous theoretical framework to a foundational problem of AI safety, namely adversarial robustness. In particular, we show that the adversarial robustness problem can be reduced to a lattice traversal problem. Each element of this lattice corresponds to an interval, i.e., an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle, containing an input point $\mathbf{x}$. Consider a multilayered perceptron classifier (MLP). An interval $I$ constitutes a sound certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and $\mathbf{x}$ can be freely perturbed in $I$ without changing the MLP's prediction. Complementarily, an interval $I$ constitutes a complete certification if $\mathbf{x} \in I$ and when $\mathbf{x}$ moves outside of $I$ the MLP's prediction is guaranteed to change. While the sound certification problem corresponds to the well-studied adversarial robustness, complete certifications have not been examined in the literature. We develop lattice traversal operators, which we apply in a refine & verify iterative scheme. Using formal MLP verifiers, sound maximality and complete minimality are guaranteed. Moreover, we examine objective optimization problems. There we discover some interesting asymmetries. For complete certifications, the minimum solution is obtained in polynomial oracle calls. This does not hold for sound certifications, where we prove strong intractability results. Additionally, we examine optimization problems in symmetric intervals (i.e., $\ell_\infty$-spheres), where we provide logarithmic algorithms. Finally, we present an empirical evaluation, using the novel ParallelepipedoNN system.

</details>


### [106] [CogniConsole: Externalizing Inference-Time Control as a Formal Abstraction for Reliable LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08774)
*Vanessa Figueiredo,Wilter Franceschi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出推理时控制（inference-time control）是影响LLM系统可靠性的关键因素，并通过CogniConsole架构和可控性探测实验证明，在固定模型架构下增加结构化脚手架可以系统性地降低输出方差和失败率，许多故障模式源于控制不足而非能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战将LLM系统可靠性仅归因于模型能力的传统观点，提出推理时控制同样重要，为超越单纯规模扩展的设计与评估提供实证基础。

Method: 提出CogniConsole架构，将控制外部化为结合程序化协调与受限提示推理的结构化接口；通过489个可控性探测在多步交互环境中进行实验，比较从非结构化到完全结构化不同脚手架水平下的输出方差和失败率。

Result: 增加结构脚手架系统性地降低了输出方差和失败率；上下文漂移和约束遵守不一致等故障模式源于控制不足而非能力不足。

Conclusion: 推理时控制应被视为一等抽象，为设计和评估LLM系统提供了超越规模扩展的新方向。

Abstract: Reliability in large language model (LLM) systems is typically framed as a function of model capability. We challenge this by demonstrating that reliability is significantly influenced by \emph{inference-time control} -- the computational layer governing task framing and context selection. We introduce \emph{CogniConsole}, an architectural instantiation that externalizes this control into a structured interface combining programmatic coordination with bounded prompt-based reasoning. Through \emph{controllability-oriented probes} ($N=489$) in a multi-step interactive environment, we show that increasing structural scaffolding -- from unstructured to fully scaffolded -- \textbf{systematically reduces output variance and failure rates under a fixed model architecture}. Our results indicate that many observed failure modes, such as context drift and inconsistent constraint adherence, arise from under-specified control rather than insufficient capability. This work provides an empirical basis for treating inference-time control as a first-class abstraction, opening new directions for designing and evaluating LLM systems beyond scaling alone.

</details>


### [107] [GATS: Graph-Augmented Tree Search with Layered World Models for Efficient Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08894)
*Maureese Williams,Dymitr Nowicki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GATS是一个将UCB1树搜索与三层世界模型结合的规划框架，在推理阶段无需LLM调用即可达到100%任务成功率，远超LATS（88.9%）和ReAct（23.9%），同时实现零方差确定性规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体规划方法（如LATS和ReAct）在规划过程中严重依赖LLM推理，导致计算成本高昂且行为具有随机性，亟需一种更高效、确定性的规划方案。

Method: 提出GATS框架，核心包括：(1) 基于UCB1的系统化树搜索；(2) 三层世界模型（L1精确符号动作匹配、L2执行日志统计学习、L3基于LLM的未知动作预测）；(3) 推理阶段完全消除LLM调用。

Result: 在合成规划任务中GATS成功率100%（LATS 92%，ReAct 64%）；在12个挑战性场景（编码工作流、网页导航、长周期任务）中GATS保持100%成功率（LATS 88.9%，ReAct 23.9%）；GATS每次任务零LLM调用（LATS 37次/任务），规划完全确定性。

Conclusion: 系统化的搜索与学习的世界模型相结合，能够在智能体规划任务中显著优于依赖LLM引导的探索方法，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低计算成本并消除随机性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise in multi-step planning tasks, but existing approaches like LATS (Language Agent Tree Search) and ReAct rely heavily on LLM inference during planning, leading to high computational costs and stochastic behavior. We present \textbf{GATS} (Graph-Augmented Tree Search), a planning framework that combines systematic UCB1-based tree search with a layered world model to eliminate LLM calls during inference while achieving superior planning performance. Our three-layer world model integrates: (L1) exact symbolic action matching, (L2) statistics learned from execution logs, and (L3) LLM-based prediction for unknown actions. On synthetic planning tasks with branching paths and dead-ends, GATS achieves \textbf{100\% success rate} compared to 92 % for LATS and 64\% for ReAct. On a comprehensive stress test spanning 12 challenging scenarios -- including coding workflows, web navigation, and long-horizon tasks -- GATS maintains \textbf{100\% success} while LATS drops to 88.9 % and ReAct to 23.9%. GATS requires \textbf{zero LLM calls per task} during planning (vs. 37 per task for LATS) and produces deterministic plans with zero variance across runs. Our results demonstrate that systematic search with learned world models can substantially outperform LLM-guided exploration for agent planning.

</details>


### [108] [Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench: Testing the Limits of Agents on Long-Horizon Terminal Tasks with Dense Reward-Based Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08964)
*Zongxia Li,Zhongzhi Li,Yucheng Shi,Ruhan Wang,Junyao Yang,Zhichao Liu,Xiyang Wu,Anhao Li,Yue Yu,Ninghao Liu,Lichao Sun,Haotao Mi,LeoweiLiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench（LHT-Bench），一个包含46个长时域任务的终端基准测试，涵盖9个类别。与现有仅评估最终结果的终端基准不同，LHT-Bench将每个任务分解为细粒度的有评分子任务，支持密集的中间奖励和部分得分。实验表明，最强模型在部分奖励阈值0.95下仅实现15.2%的pass@1，在完美奖励阈值1.0下为10.9%，平均通过率分别为4.3%和1.7%，揭示了长时域终端代理的巨大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端基准测试大多聚焦于几分钟内可完成的简单问题，仅评估最终结果，忽略了中间进度和部分解决方案，导致奖励信号稀疏且无法全面反映代理能力。因此，需要设计一个面向长时域、支持密集中间奖励的基准测试来更全面地评估AI代理。

Method: 构建包含46个长时域任务的基准测试，任务涵盖实验复现、软件工程、多模态分析、交互式游戏和科学计算等9个类别。每个任务遵循Terminal-Bench风格设置参考解决方案或模拟引擎，并进一步分解为细粒度有评分子任务，以实现密集的中间奖励和部分学分评估。

Result: 评估了15个前沿模型，代理平均每个任务消耗990万token，约231个回合和85.3分钟执行时间。最强模型在0.95部分奖励阈值下pass@1为15.2%，在1.0完美奖励阈值下为10.9%；所有模型平均通过率分别为4.3%和1.7%。

Conclusion: LHT-Bench比现有终端基准更具挑战性，当前最强模型表现仍远不理想，存在巨大改进空间。通过分析失败模式和错误类型，该基准将支持未来长时域终端代理的研究进展。

Abstract: AI agents have become capable of autonomously completing short, well-specified tasks. However, existing terminal benchmarks largely focus on simple problems that finish within minutes and are evaluated only by their final outcome. This setup overlooks intermediate progress and partial solutions, yielding sparse reward signals and an incomplete picture of agent capability. We introduce Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench, a terminal benchmark of 46 long-horizon tasks spanning nine categories, including experiment reproduction, software engineering, multimodal analysis, interactive games, and scientific computing. Each task follows a Terminal-Bench-style setup with a reference solution or simulation engine, but is further decomposed into fine-grained graded subtasks. This design enables dense intermediate rewards and partial credit, allowing evaluation to capture not only whether an agent reaches the final goal, but also how far it progresses on open-ended workflows. Tasks in Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench typically require hundreds of episodes and minutes to hours of execution, stressing long-horizon planning, long-context management, and iterative debugging rather than one-shot problem solving. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find that agents consume on average 9.9M tokens per task, with roughly 231 episodes and 85.3 minutes of execution time per run, making Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench more demanding than prior terminal-based benchmarks. Even the strongest tested model achieves 15.2% pass@1 at a partial-reward threshold of 0.95 and 10.9% at a perfect-reward threshold of 1.0, while the mean pass rate across models is 4.3% and 1.7% under the two thresholds, respectively. These results reveal headroom for improvement. We further analyze failure modes and error patterns, and release Long-Horizon-Terminal-Bench to support future progress on long-horizon terminal agents.

</details>


### [109] [A Formalization of the Mean-Field Derivation of the Vlasov Equation: AI-Assisted Lean Formalization as a Strategy Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08986)
*Joseph K. Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化数学证明的游戏框架：由数学家指导AI系统，将LaTeX文档转化为Lean 4代码，目标是编译通过、无sorry、仅依赖Lean基础公理。以非线性Vlasov方程适定性问题的完整形式化为案例（包括存在唯一性、稳定性估计、平均场极限和Lagrangian叠加原理），人类负责定义范围、分解问题和填补库缺口，AI负责执行。最终结果中约六分之一（49/299个声明）形成可被Mathlib独立吸收的自包含层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种可扩展的形式化验证方法，降低证明助理使用的门槛，使数学家能将精力集中在定理的选择和判断上，而非编写形式化证明的机械劳动。

Method: 设计形式化游戏规则：以LaTeX文档为输入，Lean 4为输出目标，编译通过且无sorry为胜利条件。人类数学家负责定义范围、分解问题、评估库缺口；AI代理执行具体的形式化证明。引入"可复用性"作为第二检查维度，衡量形式化结果能否形成自包含的通用数学层。

Result: 成功完整形式化了非线性Vlasov方程的适定性理论（存在唯一性、稳定性估计、平均场极限、弱解的Lagrangian性质）。约六分之一（49/299个声明）形成独立于Mathlib的自包含层，含22个声明的接口且无反向依赖。主要定理约一周完成，整体开发约一个月。

Conclusion: 该框架有效实现了人机协作形式化证明，AI可执行大量证明细节，数学家保留对定理正确性的判断权。形式化结果可产出能被通用库吸收的数学层。该方法论框架具有工具中立性，不依赖于特定系统。
